--- Model Response ---
To determine the shortest path between the two black nodes I and O in the given graph, we need to follow the process of examining the nodes and their connections step-by-step.

1. **Identify the black nodes:**
   - Node I
   - Node O

2. **Find the immediate neighbors of Node I:**
   - Node H
   - Node J

3. **Find the immediate neighbors of Node O:**
   - Node C
   - Node Q
   - Node R

4. **Check if there is a direct connection between Node I’s neighbors and Node O’s neighbors:**
   - Node J (neighbor of I) connects to Node G.
   - Node Q (neighbor of O) connects to Node P.
   - There is no direct connection between Node I’s neighbors and Node O’s neighbors.

5. **Expand the search to the next level of neighbors:**
   - Node H (neighbor of I) connects to Node G and T.
   - Node G (neighbor of J) connects to Node C, Node B, and Node T.
   - Node C (neighbor of O) connects to Node B, Node N, and Node G.
   - Node Q (neighbor of O) connects to Node P.
   - Node R (neighbor of O) connects to Node C.

6. **Find the shortest path from Node I to Node O:**
   - From Node I to Node H (1 edge): I -> H
   - From Node H to Node G (1 edge): H -> G
   - From Node G to Node C (1 edge): G -> C
   - From Node C to Node O (1 edge): C -> O

   Thus, the path is: I -> H -> G -> C -> O

   The length of this path is 4 edges.

7. **Evaluate other possible paths:**
   - Another potential path: I -> J -> G -> C -> O
   - The length of this path is also 4 edges.

Since both paths have the same length, the shortest path length between Node I and Node O is 4 edges.

**Answer:**
Length: 4, Path: [I, H, G, C, O]
--- Ground Truth ---

     - P -> A -> S -> Q -> I -> J
